Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in planetary theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 914 and the invariant I(A,B) = 714 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the planetary space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 914 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 714. QED.
